Fast score using FFT:
```
def fft_convolve_max(a):
   F=np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
   c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, n=nfft)[:len(a)*2-1]
   return max(c)
```

Detailed final algorithm:
```
import numpy as np, time, random? scipy.optimize

G = {}

def clip_params(params): ... using bounds.
def build_sequence(params):
   params=clip_params(np.asarray(params))
   n=G['n']; shift=params[1]
   loga = params[0]*np.log(np.arange(1,n+1)+shift-0.5?) # shift maybe param, i? Use i = np.arange(n); base = np.log(i+shift)
   Add polynomial/mode coefficients times precomputed
   # For numerical stability subtract max
   with np.errstate: a=np.exp(loga - np.max(loga))
   s=a.sum(); if s<=0 or not finite: uniform
   a/=s
   return a
def exact_score(a):
   # maybe use direct if n small; use custom with np.convolve
   c=np.convolve(a,a)
   M=c.max(); s=a.sum(); return 2*len(a)*M/(s*s)
def smooth_score_params(params):
   a=build_sequence(params)
   s=a.sum(); a=a/s
   F=rfft(a,nfft); c=irfft(F*F)[:ln]
   cs=2*n*c
   amax=cs.max()
   # soft max tau=G['tau'] (e.g. 1000)
   z=cs-amax
   # exp may under/overflow; z<=0, tau*z<= -? max 0, min -tau*large underflow ok
   m = amax + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(G['tau']*z)))/G['tau']
   return (m, a) maybe score only
```
Actually for SciPy objective, returning only score. Need cache a? not.

Objective:
```
def sequence_score_array(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if not finite or s<=0: return inf
    # if n small use direct else fft
    c=np.convolve(a,a) # direct reliable
    M=float(np.max(c))
    return float(2*n*M/(s*s))
```
For final direct. For search, use FFT-based exact? Let's implement both:
```
def fast_score_array(a, smooth=False):
    n=a.size; s=sum(a)
    a=a/s
    nfft=G['nfft']; F=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft); c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,n=nfft)[:2*n-1]
   ．cs = 2*n*c
   ．
    if smooth:
       amax=cs.max(); z=cs-amax; tau=G['tau']; val=amax + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(tau*z)))/tau
       return float(val)
    else:
       M=cs.max(); finf if nan; return float(M) # since sum=1
```
Because normalized sum=1 exactly? We divide by s, but sum after division may not exactly1; but objective custom uses sum after divide? It is exactly `2*n*M` if we treat normalized sum=1; due floating sum maybe 1+eps. Could use `s2=np.sum(a_norm)**2` but unnecessary. `make_seq_params(params)`:
```
params=np.clip(params, pmin?) maybe bounds
p=params[0]; sh=params[1]
i=G['i']
loga=p*np.log(i+sh)
if G['K']>0:
   loga = loga + params[2:] @ G['modes']

def compute_cs(a):
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s<=0: return None
   an=a/s
   F=np.fft.rfft(an, G['nfft']); c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,n=G['nfft']).real[:2*n-1]
   return 2*n*c

`active_improve(a, deadline)`:
```
best=a.astype(float).copy(); sbest=fast_score_array(best, False)
x=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300))
rng=G['rng']
no_improve=0
K=min(n, max(20, n//30))
etas=(0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2)
iter=0
while time.monotonic()<deadline:
   cs=compute_cs(best)
   if cs is None: break
   am=np.max(cs)
   # active threshold
   # take top K
   idx=np.argsort(cs)[-K:]
   # weights: maybe proportional to cs - 0.5*am
   w=cs[idx]
   wsum=w.sum()
   if wsum<=0: break
   w=w/wsum
   g=np.zeros(n)
   for kk, ww in zip(idx,w):
       k=int(kk)
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       if lo>hi: continue
       a_seg=best[lo:hi+1]
       jlo=k-lo; jhi=k-hi
       if jlo > jhi:
          # need slice from jlo down to jhi inclusive
          if jhi == 0:
             b_seg=best[jlo::-1]
          else:
             b_seg=best[jlo:jhi-1:-1]
       else:
          b_seg=best[jlo:jhi+1] # should not happen
       g[lo:hi+1] += ww * a_seg * b_seg
   gm=np.mean(g)
   g = g - gm
   gmax=np.max(np.abs(g))
   if gmax <= 1e-300: break
   g = g / gmax
   improved=False
   # maybe reduce if no_improve? scale initial eta
   for eta in etas:
       xn = x - eta*g
       anew = np.exp(xn - np.max(xn))
       sn = anew.sum()
       if sn <=0 or not finite: continue
       anew /= sn
       ss=fast_score_array(anew, False)
       if ss < sbest - 1e-12:
           best=anew; x=xn; sbest=ss; improved=True; no_improve=0; break
   if improved and time...: iter+=1
   else:
       # try smaller eta? already. Try positive direction if no improve
       for eta in (0.001,0.005,0.02,0.05):
           xn=x+eta*g; ...
       if not improve: no_improve +=1
   if no_improve>=15:
       # random perturbation to escape
       for sigma in (0.05,0.2,0.5):
           xn=x+rng.normal(0,sigma,x.size)
           anew=exp(normalize); if ss<sbest: update; break
       else: break
   if iter % 50 ==0: print score
return best, sbest
```

This can escape degenerate. Use for all iterations maybe. Good.

Helper `next_pow2(m)`:

def next_pow2(m):
    p=1
    while p<m: p*=2
    return int(p)
```
Use Python.

`init_global(n, seed)`:
```
rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
G['n']=int(n); G['rng']=rng
i=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
x=(i+0.5)/n
y=2*x-1
modes=[]; modes.append(y); modes.append(y*y); modes.append(y**3); modes.append(y**4)
for f in (1,2,3): modes.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x)); modes.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x))
G['modes']=np.vstack(modes)
G['i']=i
G['nfft']=next_pow2(2*n-1)
G['tau']=1000.0
G['bounds']=[(-0.9,0.9),(0.05,5.0)] + [(-2.0,2.0)]*(len(modes))
G['uniform']=np.ones(n)/float(n)
```
Uniform sum1.

`clip_params(params)`:
```
params=np.asarray(params,dtype float)
b=G['bounds']
if len(params)!=len(b): # pad/truncate
   out=np.zeros(len(b)); m=min(len(params),len(b)); out[:m]=params[:m]
else out=params.copy()
for idx in range(len(b)):
   if not np.isfinite(out[idx]): out[idx]=0.0
   out[idx]=min(b[idx][1], max(b[idx][0], out[idx]))
return out
```

   # To avoid overflow from positive due roundoff, clip z <=0? z max 0. If z >0 due? subtract again? Clip at 0.

best=a.copy(); sbest=fast_score_array(best,False)
x=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)); x -= np.max(x)
rng=G['rng']; n=G['n']
K=max(20, min(n, n//30))
etas=(0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4)
no=0; it=0
while time.monotonic()<deadline:
    cs=compute_cs(best)
    if cs is None: break
    am=np.max(cs)
    cand_idx=np.argsort(cs)[-min(3*K,2*n-1):]
    idx=rng.choice(cand_idx, size=min(K,len(cand_idx)), replace=False)
    w=cs[idx]
    # optional downweight very low
    thresh=0.5*am
    w=np.maximum(w-thresh, 1e-12)
    wsum=w.sum(); if wsum<=0: w=w.max+?; 
    w/=wsum
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for pos in range(len(idx)):
       k=int(idx[pos]); ww=float(w[pos])
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       a_seg=best[lo:hi+1]
       jlo=k-lo; jhi=k-hi
       if jlo>jhi:
           if jhi==0:
              b_seg=best[jlo::-1]
           else:
              b_seg=best[jlo:jhi-1:-1]
       elif jlo==jhi:
           b_seg=best[jlo:jhi+1]
       else: # if jlo<jhi (should not)
           b_seg=best[jlo:jhi+1]
       g[lo:hi+1] += ww * a_seg * b_seg
    g = g - np.mean(g)
    gscale=np.max(np.abs(g))
    if not finite or gscale<=1e-300: break
    g /= gscale
    improved=False
    # try negative then positive in separate loops? try combined list of signed
    for sign in (-1.0, 1.0):
       for eta in (0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2):
           xn = x + sign*eta*g
           anew_raw=np.exp(xn - np.max(xn))
           sraw=anew_raw.sum()
           if sraw<=0 or not finite: continue
           anew=anew_raw/sraw
           ss=fast_score_array(anew, False)
           if ss < sbest - 1e-11:
               best=anew; x=xn - np.max(xn); sbest=ss; improved=True; break
       if improved: break
    it+=1
    if improved:
       no=0
       if it % 100 ==0: print("active", it, sbest)
    else:
       no+=1
       if no >= 20:
          # try a random perturbation to escape then another gradient step? We can do random in next function; break
          break
    if sbest < 1.49: break
return best, sbest
```

```

We don't want too many repeated. Use:
```
for p in [-0.5,-0.4,-0.6,-0.3,0.0]:
   for sh in [0.5,1.0,2.0]:
       v=np.zeros(L); v[0]=p; v[1]=sh; starts.append(v)
# add small mode perturbations around -0.5,1
for m in range(min(6,K)):
   v=zeros; v[0]=-0.5; v[1]=1; v[2+m]=0.5; starts.append; v[2+m]=-0.5
# random 30
for i in range(num_random):
   v=zeros
   if i%5==0: p=-0.5 + rng.normal(0,0.05); sh=1*rng.uniform(0.5,2)
   else: p=rng.uniform(-0.8,0.3); sh=rng.uniform(0.1,4)
   v[0]=p; v[1]=sh
   amp = rng.uniform(0.2,1.0) if i%2 else rng.uniform(0.0,0.3)
   v[2:] = rng.uniform(-amp, amp, size=K)
   starts.append(clip(v))
return starts
```

`_valid_return` final:
```
def _finalize(a, fallback_n=None):
   arr=np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
   if arr.ndim !=1 or arr.size==0 or not np.isfinite(arr).all():
       arr=np.ones(fallback_n if fallback_n else 256)
   arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
   s=float(arr.sum())
   if s<0.01 or not finite:
       arr=np.ones(fallback_n if fallback_n else 256)
   return [float(v) for v in arr]
```

```python
"""
Searches for a low-peak nonnegative step-function height sequence.

Strategy:
- Use an inverse-square-root type asymptotic ansatz, which already beats the
  uniform lower candidate, and optimize it in a low-dimensional log-space
  parameterization (exponent, shift, smooth antisymmetric/symmetric modes).
- Score candidates fast with FFT-based linear convolution and a sharply
  smoothed max to make the objective differentiable for L-BFGS-B.
- Refine the best full sequence with active-peak multiplicative gradient
  steps and a small amount of randomized local perturbation.
- Always return a valid nonnegative list; direct evaluation is used at the end.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _scipy_optimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_optimize = None

G = {}


def _next_pow2(m):
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return int(p)


def _init_global(n, seed):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = (i + 0.5) / float(n)
    y = 2.0 * x - 1.0

    modes = [
        y,
        y * y,
        y ** 3,
        y ** 4,
        np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x),
        np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x),
        np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x),
        np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x),
        np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * x),
        np.cos(6.0 * np.pi * x),
    ]

    G["n"] = n
    G["rng"] = rng
    G["i"] = i
    G["modes"] = np.vstack(modes)
    G["nfft"] = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    G["tau"] = 2000.0
    G["uniform"] = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    G["bounds"] = [(-0.9, 0.9), (0.05, 5.0)] + [(-2.0, 2.0)] * len(modes)


def _clip_params(params):
    params = np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = G["bounds"]
    if params.size != len(bounds):
        out = np.zeros(len(bounds), dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(params.size, len(bounds))
        out[:m] = params[:m]
        params = out

    out = params.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    for k in range(len(bounds)):
        if not np.isfinite(out[k]):
            out[k] = 0.0
        lo, hi = bounds[k]
        if out[k] < lo:
            out[k] = lo
        elif out[k] > hi:
            out[k] = hi
    return out


def _make_seq_params(params):
    params = _clip_params(params)
    i = G["i"]
    p = float(params[0])
    sh = float(params[1])

    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore", over="ignore"):
        loga = p * np.log(i + sh)

    if params.size > 2 and G["modes"].size:
        loga = loga + params[2:] @ G["modes"]

    loga = np.where(np.isfinite(loga), loga, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(loga))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return G["uniform"].copy()

    loga = loga - mx
    loga = np.clip(loga, -50.0, 0.0)
    a = np.exp(loga)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return G["uniform"].copy()
    a /= s
    return a


def _fast_score_array(a, smooth=False):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")

    n = a.size
    finite = np.isfinite(a).all()
    if not finite:
        return float("inf")

    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")

    a = a / s
    nf = G.get("nfft", 0)
    if nf <= 0 or 2 * n - 1 > nf:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        val = float(2 * n * np.max(c))
        return float(val) if np.isfinite(val) else float("inf")

    F = np.fft.rfft(a, nf)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, n=nf).real
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    cs = 2.0 * n * c

    am = float(np.max(cs))
    if not np.isfinite(am):
        return float("inf")

    if not smooth:
        return float(am)

    tau = float(G.get("tau", 2000.0))
    z = cs - am
    z = np.minimum(z, 0.0)
    # At least one entry is zero, so this sum is safely >= 1.
    val = am + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(tau * z)))) / tau
    return float(val) if np.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


def _direct_score_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.isfinite(a).all():
        return float("inf")

    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")

    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")

    return float(2 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _param_objective(params):
    a = _make_seq_params(params)
    return _fast_score_array(a, smooth=True)


def _scipy_run(params0, maxiter):
    if _scipy_optimize is None:
        return _clip_params(params0)

    try:
        res = _scipy_optimize.minimize(
            _param_objective,
            params0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=G["bounds"],
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "ftol": 1e-9,
                "gtol": 1e-6,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        x = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return _clip_params(x)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return _clip_params(params0)


def _generate_initial_params(rng, num_random=36):
    k = int(G["modes"].shape[0])
    length = 2 + k
    starts = []

    def _add(params):
        starts.append(_clip_params(np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64)))

    fixed = [
        (-0.5, 1.0),
        (-0.5, 0.5),
        (-0.5, 0.3),
        (-0.5, 0.7),
        (-0.5, 1.5),
        (-0.5, 2.0),
        (-0.4, 1.0),
        (-0.45, 0.8),
        (-0.45, 1.3),
        (-0.55, 0.8),
        (-0.55, 1.5),
        (-0.6, 1.0),
        (-0.35, 1.0),
        (0.0, 1.0),
    ]

    for p, sh in fixed:
        v = np.zeros(length, dtype=np.float64)
        v[0] = p
        v[1] = sh
        _add(v)

    # Small mode perturbations around the inverse-square-root seed.
    for idx in range(min(8, k)):
        for val in (-1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0):
            v = np.zeros(length, dtype=np.float64)
            v[0] = -0.5
            v[1] = 1.0
            v[2 + idx] = val
            _add(v)

    for r in range(int(num_random)):
        v = np.zeros(length, dtype=np.float64)
        if r % 4 == 0:
            v[0] = -0.5 + float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05))
            v[1] = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0))
        elif r % 4 == 1:
            v[0] = float(rng.uniform(-0.8, -0.3))
            v[1] = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 3.0))
        else:
            v[0] = float(rng.uniform(-0.9, 0.4))
            v[1] = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 5.0))

        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 1.0))
        v[2:] = rng.uniform(-amp, amp, size=k)
        _add(v)

    # Remove obvious duplicate lists by tuple hashing.
    seen = set()
    out = []
    for v in starts:
        key = tuple(np.round(v, 12).tolist())
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key)
            out.append(v)
    return out


def _coord_search_params(params, deadline):
    bestp = _clip_params(params)
    besta = _make_seq_params(bestp)
    bests = _fast_score_array(besta, smooth=False)

    k = int(G["modes"].shape[0])
    steps = np.array([0.05, 0.2] + [0.1] * k, dtype=np.float64)
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        improved = False

        for d in range(bestp.size):
            trial_best = None
            trial_score = bests

            for mult in (2.0, 1.0, 0.5, -2.0, -1.0, -0.5):
                trial = bestp.copy()
                trial[d] += mult * steps[d]
                trial = _clip_params(trial)
                s = _fast_score_array(_make_seq_params(trial), smooth=False)
                if np.isfinite(s) and s < trial_score - 1e-12:
                    trial_score = float(s)
                    trial_best = trial

            if trial_best is not None:
                bestp = trial_best
                bests = trial_score
                improved = True
                no_improve = 0
                steps[d] = min(1.0, steps[d] * 1.15)
            else:
                steps[d] = max(1e-3, steps[d] * 0.7)

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve == 6:
                trial = _clip_params(bestp + G["rng"].normal(0.0, 0.12, size=bestp.size))
                s = _fast_score_array(_make_seq_params(trial), smooth=False)
                if np.isfinite(s) and s < bests - 1e-12:
                    bestp = trial
                    bests = float(s)
                    no_improve = 0
            if no_improve >= 25:
                break

        if bests < 1.45:
            break

    besta = _make_seq_params(bestp)
    return bestp, bests, besta


def _compute_cs(a):
    n = a.size
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None

    nf = G.get("nfft", 0)
    if nf <= 0 or 2 * n - 1 > nf:
        c = np.convolve(a / s, a / s)
        return 2.0 * n * c

    an = a / s
    F = np.fft.rfft(an, nf)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, n=nf).real
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    return 2.0 * n * c


def _active_refine(a, deadline):
    best = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    sbest = _fast_score_array(best, smooth=False)
    if not np.isfinite(sbest):
        best = G["uniform"].copy()
        sbest = _fast_score_array(best, smooth=False)

    n = G["n"]
    x = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
    x = np.clip(x - np.max(x), -50.0, 0.0)

    rng = G["rng"]
    k = max(20, min(n, n // 30))
    no_improve = 0
    iteration = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cs = _compute_cs(best)
        if cs is None:
            break

        am = float(np.max(cs))
        if not np.isfinite(am):
            break

        # Random subset from the hottest diagonals; this prevents the
        # symmetric plateau direction from dominating.
        pool_size = min(cs.size, 3 * k)
        pool = np.argsort(cs)[-pool_size:]
        sel_k = min(k, pool.size)
        idx = rng.choice(pool, size=sel_k, replace=False)

        w = cs[idx]
        thresh = 0.5 * am
        w = np.maximum(w - thresh, 1e-12)
        wsum = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones(sel_k, dtype=np.float64)
            w /= float(w.sum())
        else:
            w /= wsum

        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for pos in range(sel_k):
            kk = int(idx[pos])
            ww = float(w[pos])
            lo = max(0, kk - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, kk)
            if lo > hi:
                continue

            a_seg = best[lo:hi + 1]
            jlo = kk - lo
            jhi = kk - hi

            if jlo > jhi:
                if jhi == 0:
                    b_seg = best[jlo::-1]
                else:
                    b_seg = best[jlo:jhi - 1:-1]
            elif jlo == jhi:
                b_seg = best[jlo:jhi + 1]
            else:
                b_seg = best[jlo:jhi + 1]

            g[lo:hi + 1] += ww * a_seg * b_seg

        g -= np.mean(g)
        gscale = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if not np.isfinite(gscale) or gscale <= 1e-300:
            break
        g /= gscale

        improved = False
        eta_list = (0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2)

        # Mostly descent direction, but occasionally probe ascent as an
        # escape from a flat inspection point.
        signs = (-1.0, 1.0)
        for sign in signs:
            for eta in eta_list:
                xn = x + sign * eta * g
                xn = xn - np.max(xn)
                xn = np.clip(xn, -50.0, 0.0)
                an = np.exp(xn)
                sn = float(np.sum(an))
                if not np.isfinite(sn) or sn <= 0.0:
                    continue
                an /= sn
                ss = _fast_score_array(an, smooth=False)
                if np.isfinite(ss) and ss < sbest - 1e-12:
                    best = an
                    x = xn
                    sbest = float(ss)
                    improved = True
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        iteration += 1
        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
            if iteration % 50 == 0 or sbest < 1.50:
                print(f"active iterate {iteration}: score={sbest:.10f}")
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 18:
                break

        if sbest < 1.49:
            break

    return best, sbest


def _random_refine(a, deadline):
    best = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    sbest = _fast_score_array(best, smooth=False)
    n = G["n"]

    if not np.isfinite(sbest):
        best = G["uniform"].copy()
        sbest = _fast_score_array(best, smooth=False)

    x = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
    x = np.clip(x - np.max(x), -50.0, 0.0)
    rng = G["rng"]

    for sigma in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35):
        fail = 0
        it = 0
        while time.monotonic() < deadline and fail < 60 and it < 3000:
            it += 1
            xt = x.copy()
            if rng.random() < 0.5:
                xt += rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
            else:
                width = max(1, n // 200)
                start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
                xt[start:start + width] += rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=width)

            xt = xt - np.max(xt)
            xt = np.clip(xt, -50.0, 0.0)
            an = np.exp(xt)
            sn = float(np.sum(an))
            if not np.isfinite(sn) or sn <= 0.0:
                fail += 1
                continue

            an /= sn
            ss = _fast_score_array(an, smooth=False)
            if np.isfinite(ss) and ss < sbest - 1e-12:
                best = an
                x = xt
                sbest = float(ss)
                fail = 0
            else:
                fail += 1

        if sbest < 1.49:
            break

    return best, sbest


def _finalize_array(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim != 1 or arr.size == 0:
        arr = G["uniform"].copy()

    if not np.isfinite(arr).all():
        arr = G["uniform"].copy()

    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        arr = G["uniform"].copy()
        s = float(arr.sum())

    # The objective is scale invariant; keep a safe normalized scale.
    arr = arr / s
    return [float(v) for v in arr]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Return the best nonnegative height sequence found within the budget."""
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    total_s = max(30.0, float(budget_s))

    # Choose search length. If a comparable construction is provided, use its
    # length; otherwise use 4096, which keeps FFT.convolution very fast.
    raw_h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    h_len = 0
    if raw_h is not None:
        try:
            h_len = int(len(raw_h))
        except Exception:
            h_len = 0

    n = 4096
    if 1024 <= h_len <= 10000:
        n = h_len

    _init_global(n, seed)

    best_a = G["uniform"].copy()
    best_score = 2.0
    best_params = np.zeros(2 + int(G["modes"].shape[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    best_params[0] = -0.5
    best_params[1] = 1.0

    # Keep a possibly provided external construction as a fallback candidate,
    # but only evaluate it directly if its length is moderate.
    outside_list = []
    outside_score = float("inf")
    if raw_h is not None and 50 <= h_len <= 10000:
        try:
            h_arr = np.asarray(raw_h, dtype=np.float64)
            if h_arr.ndim == 1 and np.isfinite(h_arr).all():
                h_clip = np.clip(h_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
                outside_score = _direct_score_array(h_clip)
                if np.isfinite(outside_score):
                    outside_list = [float(v) for v in h_clip]
        except Exception:
            pass

    print(f"search length: {n}")
    if np.isfinite(outside_score):
        print(f"height_sequence_1 direct score: {outside_score:.10f}")

    # Phase deadlines.
    hard_deadline = start_time + total_s - 30.0

    if total_s >= 1200:
        param_deadline = start_time + total_s - 700.0
        coord_deadline = start_time + total_s - 520.0
        active_deadline = start_time + total_s - 340.0
        random_deadline = start_time + total_s - 180.0
    elif total_s >= 500:
        param_deadline = start_time + total_s - 260.0
        coord_deadline = start_time + total_s - 180.0
        active_deadline = start_time + total_s - 110.0
        random_deadline = start_time + total_s - 60.0
    else:
        param_deadline = hard_deadline
        coord_deadline = hard_deadline
        active_deadline = hard_deadline
        random_deadline = hard_deadline

    param_deadline = min(param_deadline, hard_deadline)
    coord_deadline = min(coord_deadline, hard_deadline)
    active_deadline = min(active_deadline, hard_deadline)
    random_deadline = min(random_deadline, hard_deadline)

    def _consider_seq(seq, score_hint=None):
        nonlocal_best = True  # not a closure? This is local fiber; avoid by no nested function.

    # No nested helpers; do updates inline.
    starts = _generate_initial_params(G["rng"], num_random=40)
    start_scores = []
    for p0 in starts:
        a0 = _make_seq_params(p0)
        s0 = _fast_score_array(a0, smooth=False)
        start_scores.append(float(s0) if np.isfinite(s0) else float("inf"))
        if np.isfinite(s0) and s0 < best_score:
            best_score = float(s0)
            best_a = a0
            best_params = _clip_params(p0)

    if start_scores:
        order = np.argsort(np.asarray(start_scores, dtype=np.float64))
        for pos in order:
            if time.monotonic() >= param_deadline:
                break
            p0 = starts[int(pos)]
            s_now = start_scores[int(pos)]
            if not np.isfinite(s_now) or s_now >= min(best_score, 1.60):
                # Skip obviously worse starts once we have something good.
                if best_score < 1.60 and s_now >= best_score:
                    continue

            remaining = float(param_deadline - time.monotonic())
            maxiter = 45 if remaining > 60.0 else 20
            p1 = _scipy_run(p0, maxiter)
            a1 = _make_seq_params(p1)
            s1 = _fast_score_array(a1, smooth=False)
            if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score:
                best_score = float(s1)
                best_a = a1
                best_params = _clip_params(p1)
                print(f"scipy start improved: {best_score:.10f}")

            if best_score < 1.50:
                break

    # Coordinate polish in parameter space.
    if time.monotonic() < coord_deadline and best_score > 1.495:
        p2, s2, a2 = _coord_search_params(best_params, coord_deadline)
        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
            best_score = float(s2)
            best_a = a2
            best_params = p2
            print(f"param coord improved: {best_score:.10f}")

    # Full-sequence active-peak multiplicative refinement.
    if time.monotonic() < active_deadline and best_score > 1.495:
        b3, s3 = _active_refine(best_a, active_deadline)
        if np.isfinite(s3) and s3 < best_score:
            best_score = float(s3)
            best_a = b3
            print(f"active refine improved: {best_score:.10f}")

    # Light random local search.
    if time.monotonic() < random_deadline and best_score > 1.48:
        b4, s4 = _random_refine(best_a, random_deadline)
        if np.isfinite(s4) and s4 < best_score:
            best_score = float(s4)
            best_a = b4
            print(f"random refine improved: {best_score:.10f}")

    # Rebuild from best params as well; FFT scoring may have ranked them
    # slightly differently from the stored array.
    a_from_params = _make_seq_params(best_params)
    s_from_params = _fast_score_array(a_from_params, smooth=False)
    if np.isfinite(s_from_params) and s_from_params < best_score:
        best_score = float(s_from_params)
        best_a = a_from_params

    # Direct final scoring among a few candidates.
    final_best = best_a
    final_score = float("inf")

    candidates = [best_a, a_from_params, G["uniform"]]
    if outside_list:
        candidates.append(np.asarray(outside_list, dtype=np.float64))

    for cand in candidates:
        try:
            sc = _direct_score_array(cand)
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
            final_score = float(sc)
            final_best = cand

    print(f"final best FFT score: {best_score:.10f}")
    print(f"final best direct score: {final_score:.10f}")

    return _finalize_array(final_best)
```
